# 12056 Ideas — MO Subway

*12,056 things to do in MO Subway — drive the train, be the conductor, sell tickets, help riders, clean and fix, run every station and line, the 10 subway lines to 20 stops, hundreds of train cars, platforms and trips. Starring Coach Mo, the family and Arff!*


## Drive the Subway

1. Drive the train to the next stop
2. Pull the go lever
3. Honk at the tunnel
4. Stop smoothly at the platform
5. Open the doors
6. Close the doors gently
7. Watch the signal turn green
8. Slow for a curve
9. Speed up on a straight
10. Ring the bell before you go
11. Cross the long bridge
12. Park at the depot
13. Switch to the express track
14. Follow the track lights
15. Blow the whistle

## Be the Conductor

16. Wear the conductor hat
17. Call 'All aboard!'
18. Announce the next station
19. Wave the green flag
20. Check everyone is on safely
21. Punch the tickets
22. Say 'mind the gap'
23. Do a head count
24. Welcome riders aboard
25. Thank riders leaving
26. Keep the train on time
27. Read the schedule
28. Ring the front bell
29. Give the ready signal
30. Take a uniform photo

## Ticket Booth

31. Sell a subway ticket
32. Give change (pretend)
33. Print a day pass
34. Scan a ticket at the gate
35. Open the turnstile
36. Sell a family pass
37. Refill the ticket machine
38. Help someone buy a ticket
39. Give a free kid's ticket
40. Stamp a transfer
41. Sell a gold pass
42. Count the ticket coins
43. Answer 'how much is a ride?'
44. Hand over a map
45. Recommend the day pass

## Help the Passengers

46. Help a lost rider find their stop
47. Carry a heavy bag
48. Give up your seat for Grandma
49. Help a family with a stroller
50. Point to the right platform
51. Help someone read the map
52. Find a lost teddy bear
53. Return a lost phone
54. Help a rider off the train
55. Show the way to the exit
56. Help someone in a wheelchair board
57. Calm a first-time rider
58. Walk a kid to the right car
59. Answer 'which way to the zoo?'
60. Carry a big suitcase

61. Sweep the train floor
62. Wipe the seats
63. Clean the windows
64. Empty the trash

67. Vacuum the carpets

69. Pick up litter
70. Freshen the air
71. Clean the driver's cab
72. Polish the handrails
73. Wash the outside
74. Scrub the platform
75. Make it all sparkle

## Fix the Subway

76. Fix a stuck door
77. Change a light

79. Repair a window
80. Oil a squeaky wheel
81. Fix the intercom
82. Reset a red signal
83. Patch a bumpy track
84. Fix the ticket machine
85. Replace a poster
86. Fix the escalator

88. Fix the platform gate
89. Charge the electric train
90. Fix the speaker

## Snacks on the Subway

91. Buy a pretzel
92. Get a vending snack
93. Run a coffee cart
94. Sell popcorn
95. Buy a juice
96. Open a bakery stand
97. Sell cookies
98. Get an ice cream
99. Run a newspaper stand
100. Share snacks with a friend
101. Restock the snack cart
102. Sell balloons
103. Run a candy kiosk
104. Buy a treat with coins
105. Sell fresh muffins

## Family Runs the Subway

106. Coach Mo drives the train
107. Mummy Pig sells tickets
108. Daddy is the conductor
109. Flick helps lost riders
110. Baby Evie waves from a seat
111. Arff sniffs out lost items
112. Meow naps in the station
113. The family cleans together
114. Everyone gets a uniform
115. A family photo in the cab
116. Grandma rides up front
117. The pigs run the whole line
118. Give everyone a job
119. Win 'best subway' award
120. Celebrate a million riders
